## Current Stable Baseline

- The active repository is `C:\Users\Ant\Documents\Codex\AE_agent`.
- The native CEP title/menu format is `AE Agent 2.0.0`.
- The panel is a compact dark CEP client for the local bridge daemon.
- Provider paths are separate: OpenAI API, OpenAI CLI, Gemini, Claude, OpenRouter, and Local/Ollama.
- Current operating rule: Local/Ollama remains a product provider path, but it must not be selected for planner runs, live CEP smokes, broad/default-provider smokes, fallback planning, recovery validation, or mutating validation unless the user explicitly asks for Local/Ollama in the current turn.
- Agent mode drafts structured MCP plans, validates tool names and required fields, dry-runs plans, and executes only through explicit mutation gates.
- Agent plans with `validation.ok` and review-warning classifications stay runnable in Agent and Agent Hardcore; classification is guidance, while invalid schema/tools, raw ExtendScript approval, runtime bindings, mutation permission, checkpoint, and edit-session gates remain enforced by the runner.
- Agent Hardcore is a visible composer mode next to Agent; its composer send button starts owner-mode autopilot with high reasoning, while manual `Dry run / Проверить` and `Выполнить план` controls remain visible for the latest active plan.
- The latest valid Agent plan exposes inline `Dry run / Проверить` and `Выполнить план` controls inside the chat message, while keeping the same validated backend runner and project-change gates.
- Raw ExtendScript plans stay blocked for normal Run until a successful dry run of the same current plan records a short-lived gate id; the enabled Run then sends `allowRawExtendscript:true` with the matching `rawExtendscriptDryRunId`.
- Successful raw ExtendScript fallback runs now write redacted, quarantine-only solution-candidate reports under the configured solution-candidate log directory. These candidates are not planner-visible and suggest typed-tool review rather than silent trusted reuse.
- The general `agent-scenario-openai-cli-smoke` support path is M100-aware: deterministic fallback runs through `/agents/plan/propose`, and generated render-queue cleanup first checks read-only queue status, then uses a backend proposal plus raw-ExtendScript dry-run gate only when matching generated render queue items exist.
- Agent plans or runs that reveal a typed-tool gap can create an ignored `logs/dev-requests/<id>/` bundle for a targeted Codex App dev handoff instead of continuing repo development inside the AE chat; v1 does not auto-create a Codex App chat.
- Agent planner instructions and CEP workflow presets now assume a non-technical user: presets use natural Russian plus short English variants, while tool names stay in structured plan fields, validation details, or dev evidence.
- The panel appends a compact resource report after completed chat/plan/run/dev-request operations with local five-hour task-window usage, estimated current panel context, and provider token usage when the provider returned it.
- Project-changing tools use idempotency, optional checkpoints, edit-session protection, and post-mutation verification.
- The typed bridge catalog includes production project-structure tools: `create_comp`, `create_project_folder`, `list_project_folder_items`, and `move_project_items_to_folder`.
- Common layer creation now includes a narrow `create_camera_layer` typed tool alongside text, solid, null, adjustment, shape, and project-item layer creation. Camera creation is M100-gated, supports explicit comp target or active comp, generated/default naming, point-of-interest, position, zoom, timing, idempotency/checkpoint fields, and read-back verification.
- M190 live acceptance proved the new tool matrix from inside the installed CEP panel through CDP, Agent UI, `openai-cli`/`gpt-5.5`, a panel-generated plan, dry run, protected run, bridge read-back, and generated-only cleanup. Acceptance did not use deterministic backend fallback, Local/Ollama fallback, or OpenRouter fallback.
- The typed bridge catalog now includes `create_layer_mask`, a bounded first mask-safety slice that creates one additive closed polygon mask on an existing layer, rejects destructive mask operations, and exposes mask shape/mode read-back through `get_layer_details`.
- M191 live acceptance proved the mask safety slice from inside the installed CEP panel through CDP, Agent UI, `openai-cli`/`gpt-5.5`, a panel-generated mask plan, dry run, protected run, semantic verification, bridge read-back, and generated-only cleanup. Acceptance did not use deterministic backend fallback, Local/Ollama fallback, or OpenRouter fallback.
- The typed bridge catalog now includes `duplicate_layer`, a bounded explicit single-layer duplication tool that requires a target layer index, can verify the expected source layer name in current AE stack order, returns source/duplicate read-back with layer-count post-verification, and deliberately does not delete layers or rely on selection-only ambiguity.
- The typed bridge catalog now includes `duplicate_layers`, a bounded explicit many-layer duplication runtime tool that requires non-empty concrete `layerIndices`, rejects duplicate/non-positive/out-of-range/locked/selection-only targets, returns source/duplicate pair read-back plus before/after layer counts and pair-name post-verification flags, and deliberately does not delete layers, deep-duplicate/relink source trees, or edit masks/audio/user assets.
- The typed bridge catalog now includes the M221/M225 Dakkshin runtime slice: `delete_layer` is destructive M100 and requires one explicit comp, one `layerIndex`, and one `expectedLayerName`; `set_comp_properties` targets one explicit comp and mutates only `width`, `height`, `pixelAspect`, `duration`, `frameRate`, `bgColor`, and `displayStartTime`; `set_layer_mask` creates or updates one mask on one explicit layer in one explicit comp, requires `compItemIndex` or `compName`, requires `maskIndex` for update, rejects active-comp fallback before queueing AE work, and rejects mask delete, bulk mask operations, roto, and arbitrary mask/property-tree edits.
- Planner guidance and bounded plan-repair aliases now expose `duplicate_layers` for explicit bulk/selected-layer duplicate intents. Selected-layer repair requires prior read-only selected-layer evidence before binding `{{selectedLayerIndices}}`; stack-order repair maps source names from preceding layer creation steps to AE's index-1 insertion order or inserts `get_comp_details` before ambiguous explicit duplication. Semantic verification now checks source/duplicate pair counts, expected duplicate names, current layer-index order, before/after layer counts, and post-run read-back evidence. The M207 generated-only Full UI Agent live lane is registered and fail-closed for installed-panel/CDP/OpenAI CLI validation, and M208 adds Solution Library tool-backed guidance that prefers `duplicate_layers` for explicit bulk duplication while requiring prior `get_selected_layers` evidence for selected-layer duplicate workflows.
- Semantic verification now uses mutation-specific read-back windows, parses JSON-string read-back payloads, recognizes AE property preview arrays, and the generated-only M190/M198/M207 OpenAI CLI lanes require `Outcome verification: passed` rather than read-back-gated `needs_review` overrides.
- The existing `add_layer_marker` typed tool now has explicit marker evidence: it returns created-marker read-back, `get_layer_details` exposes marker summaries, and semantic verification checks comment/time/duration against post-run read-back. It does not analyze audio, detect beats, bulk-generate markers, delete/update markers, or claim audio-derived marker evidence.
- M194 records the future marker delete/update contract as a separate gated slice. No marker delete/update runtime tools exist yet; the design requires explicit marker targeting, before/after read-back, semantic absence/update checks, and separate approval for any generated-only live validation lane.
- The typed bridge catalog now includes `update_layer_marker`, a bounded single-marker update tool that targets an existing layer marker by `markerIndex` or strict `targetTime`, optionally guards with `targetComment`, updates only comment/time/duration, and returns before/after marker read-back.
- The typed bridge catalog now includes `delete_layer_marker`, a bounded single-marker delete tool that targets an existing layer marker by `markerIndex` or strict `targetTime`, optionally guards with `targetComment`, returns deleted-marker read-back plus after-summary, and semantically verifies the deleted marker is absent after read-back.
- M220 freezes the next risky Dakkshin typed-tool contract before implementation: future `delete_layer` must be destructive M100 with explicit single-layer and expected-name guards; future `set_comp_properties` may update only width, height, pixelAspect, duration, frameRate, bgColor, and displayStartTime on one explicit comp; future `set_layer_mask` may only create/update one explicit layer/mask target with bounded mask fields; and the future live lane must be parent-owned, generated-only, fail-closed, and restricted to OpenAI CLI through the installed panel when available.
- M221 implements the local runtime/catalog layer for that contract without CEP UI edits or live AE/CEP mutation: the bridge exposes `delete_layer`, `set_comp_properties`, and `set_layer_mask` to the typed tool catalog and planner-visible list, classifies `delete_layer` as destructive M100, blocks direct unsafe `delete_layer` calls, and keeps planner repair, semantic verification, and generated-only live acceptance for later milestones.
- M222 adds local planner and semantic coverage for the M221 Dakkshin tools without CEP/live validation: planner guidance requires inspection/read-back, delete aliases repair only with prior comp/layer inspection plus explicit `layerIndex` and `expectedLayerName`, comp/mask aliases normalize only bounded fields, and semantic verification now fails closed without post-run read-back for delete absence, comp property updates, and mask create/update.
- M223 wires the generated-only Full UI Agent OpenAI CLI lane command `full-ui-agent-dakkshin-typed-tools-openai-cli-smoke` for the M221/M222 Dakkshin tools. The lane requires panel Agent plans, OpenAI CLI auth mode, final bridge read-back, passed semantic verification, generated-prefix cleanup, and fail-closed rejection of deterministic fallback, Local/Ollama, OpenRouter, missing read-back, or generated leftovers; M223 did not run the mutating live lane.
- M224 local pre-live validation was refreshed after M227 and passed for the generated-only Dakkshin lane. Earlier parent-owned live reruns reached OpenAI CLI panel planning, dry run, protected run, bridge read-back, and generated-prefix cleanup, but stopped fail-closed before M227 fixed live AE evidence normalization. The M224 queue item remains `queued` with no `completedBy`; only the parent supervisor may run the declared live command after read-only installed-panel CDP inspect and connector-status smoke pass.
- M227 normalizes live AE semantic read-back evidence for Dakkshin without weakening gates: composition `bgColor` accepts only AE color-channel quantization, mask opacity/feather/expansion accept AE property preview wrappers, and `delete_layer`, `set_comp_properties`, and `set_layer_mask` still require mutation-scoped post-run read-back before later risky mutations.
- The generated-only Dakkshin lane fixture now places read-back windows after `set_comp_properties`, after `delete_layer`, and after `set_layer_mask` create before the next risky mutation. The lane remains OpenAI CLI, panel-plan, dry-run, protected-run, semantic-verification, generated-cleanup, and leftover-detection gated.
- New project-structure mutations are M100-gated and inherit checkpoint/idempotency/post-verification safety fields; the folder listing tool remains read-only.
- Precomp/source workflows include `deep_duplicate_precomp_sources` for recursively duplicating a selected precomp layer's source comp and nested comp/footage project items without raw ExtendScript in Agent plans.
- After `deep_duplicate_precomp_sources`, selected-precomp parent-layer read-back is repair-guarded: `get_layer_details` with `{{selectedPrecompLayerIndex}}` is bound to `steps.<deep-duplicate-step>.result.comp.itemIndex`, so reading the duplicated source comp cannot accidentally redirect the later layer check into a comp with a different layer stack.
- Raw ExtendScript remains available as an escape hatch, but normal product workflows should use typed bridge tools.
- The Solution Library includes Dakkshin-inspired advisory typed-plan recipes for basic comp setup, safe effect addition, and selected-layer animation, plus tool-backed `duplicate_layers` guidance for explicit bulk/selected-layer duplicate workflows; these are planner hints only and still execute through normal Agent validation and protected run gates.
- CEP `Reload` forces a cache-busted reload of installed `index.html` and passes a fresh asset nonce to CSS/JS, so an already-open panel can pick up synced files without stale `panel.js?v=<old>` cache entries.
- CEP install/sync clears only this extension's Chromium cache folders (`Cache`, `Code Cache`, `GPUCache`, `blob_storage`) while preserving Local Storage.
- Installed CEP tracked files now match the repository at `AE Agent 2.0.0`.
- Live AE/CEP connectivity with the opened `AE Agent 2.0.0` panel is verified: bridge status, `ping_ae`, CDP inspect, and connector-status smoke pass.
- Standing project rule as of M198: when After Effects, the installed AE Agent panel, and bridge are available, live CEP/AE validation is mandatory; planner-visible or mutating tool changes also require a relevant generated-only Full UI Agent `openai-cli` planner acceptance lane, or a newly added narrow lane before milestone closeout.
- Historical M182 note: the broad local-Ollama CDP smoke once passed through plan generation, saved-plan recovery, dry run, and read-only run. Current validation must not rerun Local/Ollama or broad default-provider smokes without explicit user direction. Recovery of saved structured Agent plans remains deterministic: the panel stores only the structured plan in chat history, then asks the bridge `/agents/plan/propose` endpoint to revalidate it and mint a fresh M100 action proposal instead of re-prompting the local planner.

## Current SDK Baseline

- Current SDK orchestrator work is local-gated and AE Agent-specific until a separate milestone proves equivalent fail-closed behavior in the sibling `codex-sdk-orchestrator-tool`.
- Current production-code SDK write readiness is limited to exactly `scripts/provider-api-smoke.js` and `scripts/provider-contract-smoke.js`.
- General SDK autopilot repo edits, broad production-code writes, CEP-panel SDK writes, external-provider/OpenAI CLI planner validation, mutating-live validation outside the approved M188/M190/M191 generated-only live lanes, package installs, dependency changes, push, and PR creation remain out of scope without fresh bounded approval.
- CEP-panel SDK writes remain disabled; `cep-panel/panel.js` must not be edited through the SDK lane.
- Current M152+ governance/readiness/conveyor/extraction evidence is directly checked through `npm.cmd run check:rules` and summarized in `.codex-audit/sdk-current-state.json`.
- Historical SDK proof references are summarized in `.codex-audit/sdk-history-index.json`; old proof packets should not be reread by default unless a specific audit needs them.
- SDK smoke consolidation is planned in `.codex-audit/sdk-smoke-consolidation-plan.json`; existing milestone-specific smokes stay active until replacement coverage is green.
- The AE Agent cleanup conveyor command captures full child stdout/stderr into ignored `.codex-runtime/sdk/cleanup-conveyor-logs` execution logs by default, prints compact terminal summaries, and validates both working-tree changes and commits created since pre-run `HEAD` against the selected planned-path allowlist.
- The AE Agent feature conveyor is now locally smoke-checked for Dakkshin intake, M187 field validation, M190 Full UI Agent new-tool validation, and M191 mask-safety validation through `.codex-audit/sdk-feature-conveyor/184-dakkshin-intake-feature-queue.json` and `orchestrator/run-ae-agent-feature-conveyor.mjs`; SDK workspace-write execution remains separate from the M188/M190/M191 `--validate-live` lanes.
- The AE Agent roadmap supervisor now sits above existing bounded runners as a deterministic local autopilot: plan-only preview, queueSha256-bound single approval for `roadmap-sdk` chains, SDK writer path plus `--engine cli` writer fallback for SDK parser/transport failures, read-only `--live-check` against an already-open AE Agent panel, execute-one, run-until-budget, runtime session state under `.codex-runtime/sdk/roadmap-supervisor/`, one writer child at a time, up to two read-only reviewers in parallel, validation before auto-commit, optional item-level generated-only live validation with single-approval live bindings or fallback `--live-approval-text`, required handoff checks, exact approval text, and no push/dependency/mutating-live/CEP/external-provider work by default. M209 records duplicate_layers queue evidence that plan-only creates no child run, SDK thread, or runtime state, and that supervisor smokes cover single-approval queue behavior. M210 records duplicate_layers closeout docs/governance state with no runtime behavior, CEP panel, dependency, push, PR, or live AE/CEP changes during closeout, M211 reconciles the tracked closeout state with the parent-updated `.codex/handoff.md`, the AO-inspired support lane now uses AUX-001 for activity/session intake, AUX-002 for stuck/escalation design, AUX-003 for the static dashboard, and AUX-004 for the read-only failure analyzer; historical M212-M215 labels remain compatibility records only and do not define future support work. Future orchestrator/support-lane work should use AUX labels instead of new product-roadmap M### ids; supervisor queue items may now use explicit `AUX-###` labels without a numeric `milestone`, and supervisor plan/handoff output preserves that label instead of synthesizing an `M###`.
- The roadmap supervisor Mission Runner now supports `--mission-plan-only` and `--mission-run` as a universal approval layer for "do it through the supervisor" work. Mission approval binds mission/queue hashes, item/time budgets, bounded follow-up permission, generated-only OpenAI CLI live policy, live bindings, no push, no dependency changes, and no user asset mutation. Existing queues can run without a manual plan-only copy/paste step; prompt missions synthesize and separately commit a tracked queue artifact before execution. Mission runtime state records phases from intake through queue creation, plan verification, item/reviewer/validation/live work, bounded follow-up, handoff, commit, and stop. If a writer child exits with the known SDK parser/process-termination envelope after producing non-empty changes strictly within `plannedPaths`, the supervisor now records `writer_parser_parent_finalize`, runs normal validation/handoff/commit gates, and does not start a dirty-tree follow-up.
- AUX-014 starts a generic repository tool-importer support lane as internal tooling, not product behavior. The control-plane artifacts define a one-command resumable importer manifest, ignored run-state layout under `.codex-runtime/sdk/generic-repo-importer/<run-id>/`, parallel analysis and implementation worktree phases, a controlled merge supervisor, non-live validation before any live queue, and a serial generated-only OpenAI CLI AE/CEP acceptance queue using M100 proposal, dry run, confirmed run, read-back, and semantic verification. The first slice does not implement the runner, create worktrees, run live AE/CEP, use Local/Ollama, change dependencies, push, or create a PR.
- AUX-015 adds the first generic importer command skeleton at `orchestrator/run-generic-repo-tool-importer.mjs`. It accepts `--manifest`, validates `generic-repo-tool-importer.manifest.v1` against the AUX-014 contract, requires Codex CLI semantics with web search disabled and Local/Ollama rejected, checks the target git tree is clean or owned, creates only the ignored `.codex-runtime/sdk/generic-repo-importer/<run-id>/` root, writes `state.json`, `events.jsonl`, `manifest.original.json`, `manifest.normalized.json`, and `supervisor-plan.json`, then stops before analysis. It does not create branches, worktrees, child runs, live AE/CEP runs, dependency/package changes, product runtime edits, push, PR, or GitHub automation.
- AUX-016 extends the same generic importer command with explicit `--run-analysis`. The analysis phase reads only a local fixture/copied source inventory, writes ignored durable artifacts under `analysis/` (`repo-fingerprint.json`, `tool-candidates/*.json`, `automation-candidates/*.json`, `risk-map.json`, `read-back-requirements.json`, and `batch-plan.json`), updates `state.json`, `events.jsonl`, and `supervisor-plan.json`, verifies completed outputs on resume, and stops before implementation worktrees. It rejects Local/Ollama through manifest validation, named-repo assumptions during analysis, scoped secrets, required-license misses, and missing analysis outputs. It still creates no worktrees, child runs, live AE/CEP runs, package/dependency changes, product runtime edits, push, PR, or GitHub automation.
- AUX-017 extends the generic importer with explicit `--plan-implementation`. The planner resumes only from completed AUX-016 analysis, verifies required analysis outputs, writes ignored durable artifacts under `implementation/` (`batch-worktree-plan.json`, `planned-paths.json`, and `batch-prompts/*.md`), updates `state.json`, `events.jsonl`, and `supervisor-plan.json`, and stops at `currentPhase:"implementation_planned"` / `nextPhase:"implementation_worktrees"` without creating real branches, worktrees, or Codex child runs. It enforces planned-path allowlists, forbidden paths, package/dependency-change rejection unless explicitly allowed by manifest, shared-file conflict ownership, named-repo/Local-Ollama boundaries, missing-output fail-closed behavior, and resume verification.
- AUX-018 extends the generic importer with explicit `--plan-merge`. The merge supervisor resumes only from completed AUX-017 implementation planning, verifies required analysis and implementation outputs, writes ignored durable artifacts under `merge/` and `validation/` (`supervisor-merge-plan.json`, `accepted-batches.json`, `rejected-batches.json`, and `non-live-report.json`), updates `state.json`, `events.jsonl`, and `supervisor-plan.json`, and stops at `currentPhase:"merge_planned"` / `nextPhase:"live_queue_planning"` without applying changes or running validation commands. It enforces planned-path-only fixture results, forbidden paths, dependency/package-change rejection unless explicitly allowed by manifest, shared-file conflict ownership, dirty-tree ownership through the existing target clean gate, named-repo/Local-Ollama boundaries, missing-output fail-closed behavior, and resume verification.
- AUX-019 extends the generic importer with explicit `--plan-live-queue`. The live-queue planner resumes only from completed AUX-018 merge planning, verifies required analysis, implementation, and merge outputs, writes ignored durable fixture evidence under `live-queue/` and `locks/` (`queue.json`, `locks/live-ae-cep.lock`, and per-item M100 proposal, dry-run, confirmed-run, read-back, semantic-verification, and cleanup-proof JSON artifacts), updates `state.json`, `events.jsonl`, and `supervisor-plan.json`, and stops at `currentPhase:"live_queue_planned"` / `nextPhase:"completed"` without opening AE/CEP, invoking OpenAI CLI, or mutating user assets. It enforces lock availability, M100 proposal presence, dry-run/confirmed-run proposal matching, read-back presence, semantic pass status, generated-prefix cleanup proof, Local/Ollama/fallback-provider evidence rejection, missing-output fail-closed behavior, and resume verification.
- AUX-020 adds the first approved implementation-worktree runner boundary. `--run-implementation-worktrees` resumes from completed AUX-017 implementation planning, verifies upstream artifacts, creates only detached importer-owned worktrees under `.codex-runtime/sdk/generic-repo-importer/<run-id>/worktrees/`, records child-run intent and batch-result evidence, updates state/supervisor-plan, and stops at `currentPhase:"implementation_worktrees_ready"` / `nextPhase:"implementation_child_runs"` before Codex child runs, source merge application, validation command execution, live AE/CEP/CDP/OpenAI CLI planner validation, product runtime edits, dependency/package changes, push/PR/GitHub automation, or user-asset mutation.
- The M216-M219 manual typed-tools queue is committed as `fd4e39b`; AUX-009/AUX-011 repaired the read-only reviewer parser/process-termination blockers without retrying product work. M216 completed as a coverage-only item, M217 repaired the binding/schema subset, and M218 repaired duplicate layer-order/final read-back expectations: `folder.itemIndex` can satisfy folder target bindings, marker/mask/camera comp aliases normalize into `compItemIndex`, `find_project_items` exact-name strings become `query` plus `exactName:true`, `itemType:"composition"` normalizes to `type:"comp"`, `duplicate_layers` source names follow AE stack order after index-1 layer insertion, and semantic verification fails closed on pair/name/read-back order mismatch. ### Milestone 221 Queue Item: Dakkshin Runtime Typed Bridge Tools

- Added `delete_layer` in `mcp-server/bridge-daemon.js` as a destructive M100 typed tool for exactly one explicit comp/layer target. It requires `layerIndex` and non-empty `expectedLayerName`, rejects active-selection/bulk delete fields, rejects locked/out-of-range/name-mismatch targets, removes one layer inside an undo group, and returns deleted-layer payload, layer count before/after, same-name count, original-index read-back, and absence-friendly `postVerification` fields.
- Added `set_comp_properties` as a narrow explicit-comp mutation that rejects empty updates, unsupported fields, and invalid values; it mutates only `width`, `height`, `pixelAspect`, `duration`, `frameRate`, `bgColor`, and `displayStartTime`, with before/after comp read-back and per-field `postVerification`.
- Added `set_layer_mask` as a bounded create/update mask tool for one explicit layer. Create requires vertices; update requires one `maskIndex` and rejects rename/delete/bulk/roto fields. The tool supports bounded vertices, `maskMode`, `inverted`, `opacity`, `feather`, and `expansion`, and returns before/after mask read-back plus `postVerification`.
- Exposed all three tools in the bridge tool catalog, planner-visible list, mutation registries, ChatGPT connector write-tool deny surface, and solution-promotion helper typed-tool lists. `delete_layer` is registered in `M100_DESTRUCTIVE_TOOL_NAMES`.

- 2026-05-22: M174-M177 are local-only cleanup conveyor work. They do not change CEP panel, bridge, provider, AE runtime behavior, SDKThread/network policy, dependencies, push, or PR state.
- 2026-05-22: The active roadmap came from the M173 cleanup conveyor sequence: M174 roadmap active-state split, M175 runtime artifact cleanup note, M176 SDK current/history split, and M177 SDK smoke consolidation plan.
- 2026-05-22: M173 charges the four AE Agent cleanup stages into the SDK milestone conveyor as local-only queued work, not as broad execution approval. Each item keeps `maxSdkThreadRuns:0`, no live CEP/AE, no runtime deletion/move, no dependency change, no push, and no PR without fresh explicit approval.
- 2026-05-22: M172 defines a future standalone adapter/plugin interface, but AE Agent governance packet validators, current M152+ direct checks, package script assertions, README assertions, and project evidence policy remain project-local until a separate implementation milestone proves equivalent fail-closed behavior.
- 2026-05-22: M165 historical archive cleanup moved only M164-reviewed candidates into `.codex-audit/sdk-history-archive/`; current M152+ evidence, active command files, provider smoke targets, `package.json`/`package-lock.json`, and `cep-panel/panel.js` remain in place.
- 2026-05-21: M152 supersedes the production-code SDK write allowlist with exactly `scripts/provider-api-smoke.js` and `scripts/provider-contract-smoke.js`; general SDK workflow, production-code outside those files, CEP-panel writes, external-provider/OpenAI CLI planner validation, live CEP/AE validation, mutating-live validation, package installs, and dependency changes remain outside the claim.
- 2026-05-13: ChatGPT subscription access uses Codex CLI auth, not a normal OpenAI API key.
- 2026-05-13: OpenAI API key access remains separate and uses normal API billing.
- 2026-05-13: Gemini and Claude use provider API keys and official HTTP APIs.
- 2026-05-13: No Pro/license gate is part of AE Agent.
- 2026-05-13: No production dependencies are added unless a milestone records a concrete reason.
- 2026-05-13: Agent plans should prefer typed bridge tools and avoid raw ExtendScript for common workflows.
- 2026-05-13: New mutating tools must join the existing checkpoint/idempotency/verification model.

### Milestone 195

- Added `update_layer_marker` to `mcp-server/bridge-daemon.js` as a narrow M100-gated mutating typed tool for one existing layer marker.
- The tool targets active comp, `compItemIndex`, or `compName`; requires `layerIndex`; accepts `markerIndex` or strict `targetTime`; optionally validates `targetComment`; updates only `comment`, `time`, and `duration`; rejects missing, ambiguous, out-of-range, locked-layer, negative-time, and negative-duration cases.

### AUX-076 Full-Intake Parent Output Bloat Guard

Progress:
- Added `--compact-json` to `orchestrator/run-generic-repo-full-intake.mjs` for parent-facing full-intake output. The compact contract prints bounded status/counts, last item, commit ids, requeued ids, failed ids, compact resolution-family counts/affected ids, next action, and compact follow-up paths/commands.
- Added `orchestrator/full-intake-ledger-summary.mjs` as a durable-ledger-only summary command for compact family counts, queued `live_lane_needed` ids, failed ids/reasons, and terminal status counts without runtime JSON.
- Extended full-intake smoke coverage and added `scripts/full-intake-ledger-summary-smoke.js` to prove compact output does not include full `resolutionQueue.tickets`, child stdout/stderr, transcripts, prompts, importer result payloads, batch reports, or runtime state.

Decision Log:
- Kept legacy `--json` output unchanged for existing machine consumers and made the parent-safe path explicit as `--compact-json`.
- The recommended long-run command flow after live/import runs is now: `node orchestrator/run-generic-repo-full-intake.mjs ... --compact-json`, then `node orchestrator/full-intake-status.mjs --run-id <id> --compact --event-limit 8 --batch-limit 1`, then `node orchestrator/full-intake-ledger-summary.mjs --ledger <ledger> --compact`.
- Parent chats should not paste or inspect full runtime `state.json`, `run-report.json`, queue `batch-report.json`, child stdout/stderr, transcripts, prompts, importer result objects, or broad summaries unless a later narrow debugging milestone explicitly names a blocker and records why.
